windows.reflective_load.ReflectiveLoad
No reflectively loaded code found — 0 detections

The detector ran and returned no rows. Nothing in this capture matched all three conditions the reflective-load signature requires:

  1. a memory region carrying a valid MZ/PE header
  2. absent from every one of the process's loader lists (InLoad / InInit / InMem)
  3. no backing file on disk

Scope: 108 process(es) were present in this capture and were examined.

How to read this. For a benign-activity capture this is the expected, clean result. For a capture taken during a known attack it is a genuine miss, and should be reported as one.

Caveat. A null result is only evidence if the activity under test was actually present in this capture, and if the detector is known to fire on positive controls. Confirm both before citing this as evidence of absence.
